# Agents · S · part 3

966 entries, sami-proxy to scaffold-engineer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sami-proxy**: Use before a coding agent asks the user for a decision, approval, clarification, or permission to continue. Consult with the proposed question, original goal, relevant user messages, evidence, and any standing approvals. ([sjawhar/dotfiles](https://github.com/sjawhar/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/omp/agents/sami-proxy.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **samizdat-conventions-reviewer**: Reviews changed Perl/template code against Samizdat's architectural conventions (MVC layering, model ownership, route naming, template naming, dbtype, i18n). Use after implementing or modifying a feature, before considering it done. ([FAKEMedium/Samizdat](https://github.com/FAKEMedium/Samizdat/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/samizdat-conventions-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **saml-configuration**: Configure SAML Single Sign-On (SSO) by uploading Identity Provider (IdP) metadata for secure authentication integration. ([DataDog/pup](https://github.com/DataDog/pup/blob/HEAD/agents/saml-configuration.md) · ★1k · 2 copies)
- **Sammy**: Use when: a feature is built and needs a security review before merge, when Olie wants a full-system security audit, when responding to a suspected vulnerability, when reviewing a third-party dependency, or when designing/auditing authentication, authorization, secrets handling, input validation, data exposure, or supply-chain security. Sammy is the security expert. Reads code with the eyes of an attacker; understands the bigger picture before judging a permission or a sanitiser; fixes what is fixable now and annotates what isn't. Does not write product features. ([aminfara/dotfiles](https://github.com/aminfara/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/SammySecurity.agent.md))
- **sample**: Writes runnable code samples and tutorials that get developers to first success fast — real values, error handling, expected output, language parity. - **sast-flow-tracer**: Traces data flow from entry points to dangerous operations. Cross-file reasoning to determine which entries can reach which dangers, and what validation exists in between. Inherits commit/push discipline from sb-ingestor. Invoked by the sb-compile orchestrator, not user-invocable. - **sb-data-analyst**: Data and analytics specialist. ([contextosai/SecondBrain-collab](https://github.com/contextosai/SecondBrain-collab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sb-data-analyst.md) · ★3)

- **sb-distiller**: Rewrites strictlybetter's inheritance body from the ledger for a cold-start reader. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run every distill_every experiments and by /strictlybetter:distill. Fresh-context: reads the ledger, the current body, and the engine's stats JSON, never the conversation; every claim points at ledger ids; writes only to the output path. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-distiller.md) · ★1)
- **sb-docker-reviewer**: Reviews changed Spring Boot container, Compose and CI artifacts for demonstrated packaging, startup, persistence, resource, permissions and verification/publication defects. Read-only; evidence over checklists. ([alefiengo/spring-boot-tools](https://github.com/alefiengo/spring-boot-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-docker-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **sb-experimenter-high**: strictlybetter experimenter pinned to HIGH reasoning effort. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for the concurrency, model, refactor-enabling, and dependency operators: parallelize, change an architecture or objective, structural moves, library swaps. Fresh-context per experiment: implements exactly one pre-registered hypothesis inside one worktree, sees no other experiment, no holdout, no confirm numbers; returns DONE &lt;id&gt; or BLOCKED: &lt;reason&gt;. Byte-identical to the other sb-experimenter agents except this frontmatter. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-experimenter-high.md) · ★1)
- **sb-experimenter-low**: strictlybetter experimenter pinned to LOW reasoning effort. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for the config, docs, and test-add operators: knob and flag changes, documentation, adding a test that captures a case. Fresh-context per experiment: implements exactly one pre-registered hypothesis inside one worktree, sees no other experiment, no holdout, no confirm numbers; returns DONE &lt;id&gt; or BLOCKED: &lt;reason&gt;. Byte-identical to the other sb-experimenter agents except this frontmatter. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-experimenter-low.md) · ★1)
- **sb-experimenter-medium**: strictlybetter experimenter pinned to MEDIUM reasoning effort. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for the algorithmic, allocation, caching, bugfix, numerics, and data operators: replace an algorithm or data structure in one function, remove copies, memoize, fix a root cause. Fresh-context per experiment: implements exactly one pre-registered hypothesis inside one worktree, sees no other experiment, no holdout, no confirm numbers; returns DONE &lt;id&gt; or BLOCKED: &lt;reason&gt;. Byte-identical to the other sb-experimenter agents except this frontmatter. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-experimenter-medium.md) · ★1)
- **sb-ingestor**: Ingests a single source (X URL, article URL, PDF path, YouTube URL, file, or pasted text) into the user's knowledge base. Runs the full /sb ingest workflow end-to-end — fetch, extract, write to raw/ with proper frontmatter, then commit (and push, if the KB has a remote). Does NOT compile into wiki pages — that is /sb compile's job. Use when the user says "ingest this", "save this to my second brain", "add this to my knowledge base", or pastes a URL meant for the knowledge base. Returns the saved file path, a one-line summary, and the commit SHA. ([ColoMiranda/second-brain](https://github.com/ColoMiranda/second-brain/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-ingestor.md))
- **sb-judge**: Blind judge for strictlybetter. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for every promoted experiment before confirmation.
